		{"Steel Blue", {26, 31, 36}, {36, 44, 51}, {49, 59, 69}, {62, 74, 87}, {90, 150, 210}},
		{"Sunrise", {36, 31, 23}, {51, 45, 32}, {69, 60, 44}, {87, 76, 55}, {255, 190, 70}},
		{"Violet Storm", {27, 25, 36}, {39, 35, 51}, {52, 48, 69}, {66, 60, 87}, {120, 90, 230}},
		{"Emerald Night", {21, 36, 30}, {30, 51, 43}, {41, 69, 57}, {52, 87, 72}, {40, 210, 140}},
	}
	local PAL_AUTO = "Auto (Theme)"
	local palOpts, schemeMap = {PAL_AUTO}, {}
	for _, s in schemes do
		local function c3(t) return Color3.fromRGB(t[1], t[2], t[3]) end
		table.insert(palOpts, s[1])
		schemeMap[s[1]] = {bg = c3(s[2]), cd = c3(s[3]), hv = c3(s[4]), pr = c3(s[5]), ac = c3(s[6])}
	end

	local function currentAccent()
		local th = Flags.th or "Blue"
		if th == "Custom" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(
				math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.cr) or 33, 0, 255),
				math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.cg) or 150, 0, 255),
				math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.cb) or 243, 0, 255))
		end
		return pMap[th] or pMap.Blue
	end
	local function isScheme()
		local p = Flags.pal
		return p ~= nil and p ~= PAL_AUTO and schemeMap[p] ~= nil
	end
	local function baseTheme()
		if isScheme() then return schemeMap[Flags.pal] end
		return derive(currentAccent())
	end

	-- Blend: second colour (harmony of the accent, a preset, or custom RGB)
	local harmony = {"Complementary", "Analogous", "Analogous Left", "Analogous Wide", "Triadic", "Triadic Left", "Split-Complementary", "Split Left", "Tetradic", "Square", "Neon Complement", "Lighter", "Darker", "Muted", "Vivid"}
	local blendOpts = {}
	for _, n in harmony do table.insert(blendOpts, n) end
	for _, p in presets do table.insert(blendOpts, p[1]) end
	table.insert(blendOpts, "Custom")
	local function blendB(a)
		local v = Flags.bc2 or "Complementary"
		local h, s, vv = a:ToHSV()
		if v == "Complementary" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.5) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Analogous" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.083) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Triadic" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.333) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Split-Complementary" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.417) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Analogous Left" then return Color3.fromHSV((h - 0.083) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Analogous Wide" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.167) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Triadic Left" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.667) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Split Left" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.583) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Tetradic" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.167) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Square" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.25) % 1, s, vv)
		elseif v == "Neon Complement" then return Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.5) % 1, 1, 1)
		elseif v == "Lighter" then return Color3.fromHSV(h, math.clamp(s * 0.45, 0, 1), 1)
		elseif v == "Darker" then return Color3.fromHSV(h, s, vv * 0.45)
		elseif v == "Muted" then return Color3.fromHSV(h, s * 0.35, vv * 0.85)
		elseif v == "Vivid" then return Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
		elseif v == "Custom" then
			return Color3.fromRGB(
				math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.br) or 255, 0, 255),
				math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.bgn) or 80, 0, 255),
				math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.bb) or 160, 0, 255))
		end
		return pMap[v] or Color3.fromHSV((h + 0.5) % 1, s, vv)
	end

	-- Effect engine: keeps a registry of every themed property and repaints it each tick.
	local FX = {on = false, reg = {}, look = {}, base = nil, lastScan = 0, lastTick = 0, dirty = true}
	local function fxScan(now)
		for k, v in FX.look do
			if now - v[2] > 3 then FX.look[k] = nil end
		end
		local added = 0
		for _, root in {gw.Gui, hud} do
			for _, d in root:GetDescendants() do
				if not FX.reg[d] and not d:GetAttribute("NoTheme") then
					local e
					if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
						local v = FX.look[key(d.BackgroundColor3)]
						if v then e = e or {}; e.bg = v[1] end
					end
					if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
						local v = FX.look[key(d.TextColor3)]
						if v then e = e or {}; e.tx = v[1] end
					end
					if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
						local v = FX.look[key(d.Color)]
						if v then e = e or {}; e.st = v[1] end
					end
					if e then FX.reg[d] = e; added += 1 end
				end
			end
		end
		return added
	end
	local function fxPaint(rc3, perEl)
		for d, e in FX.reg do
			if d.Parent == nil then
				FX.reg[d] = nil
			else
				if e.bg then d.BackgroundColor3 = perEl and perEl(e.bg, d) or rc3[e.bg] end
				if e.tx then d.TextColor3 = perEl and perEl(e.tx, d) or rc3[e.tx] end
				if e.st then d.Color = perEl and perEl(e.st, d) or rc3[e.st] end
			end
		end
	end
	local function fxStart()
		if FX.on then return end
		FX.on = true
		local now = os.clock()
		for _, r in names do FX.look[key(cT[r])] = {r, now} end
		fxScan(now)
		FX.lastScan, FX.dirty = now, true
	end
	local function fxStop()
		if not FX.on then return end
		fxScan(os.clock())
		local base = FX.base or baseTheme()
		fxPaint(base, nil)
		for _, r in names do cT[r] = base[r] end
		FX.on, FX.reg, FX.look = false, {}, {}
	end
	local function fxRender(now)
		local mode = Flags.fx or "Off"
		local base = FX.base
		if not base then base = baseTheme(); FX.base = base end
		local sch = isScheme()
		local spd = math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.fxs) or 30, 1, 100) / 100
		local t = now * spd * 0.5
		local animated = true
		local rc3 = {bg = base.bg, cd = base.cd, hv = base.hv, pr = base.pr, ac = base.ac}
		local perEl
		local mn = gw.Main
		local mp, ms = mn.AbsolutePosition, mn.AbsoluteSize
		local function uAt(d)
			local o = d:IsA("UIStroke") and d.Parent or d
			if not o or not o:IsA("GuiBase2d") then return 0 end
			local p = o.AbsolutePosition
			return math.clamp((p.X - mp.X) / math.max(ms.X, 1), 0, 1) * 0.75
				+ math.clamp((p.Y - mp.Y) / math.max(ms.Y, 1), 0, 1) * 0.25
		end
		if mode == "RGB Cycle" then
			local ac = Color3.fromHSV(t % 1, 0.8, 1)
			rc3.ac = ac
			if not sch then
				local n = derive(ac)
				rc3.bg, rc3.cd, rc3.hv, rc3.pr = n.bg, n.cd, n.hv, n.pr
			end
		elseif mode == "Rainbow Wave" then
			rc3.ac = Color3.fromHSV(t % 1, 0.8, 1)
			perEl = function(role, d)
				if role == "ac" then return Color3.fromHSV((t - uAt(d) * 0.9) % 1, 0.8, 1) end
			end
		elseif mode == "Blend 2 Colors" then
			local a = base.ac
			local b = blendB(a)
			local anim = Flags.fxa == true
			animated = anim
			local sh = anim and t * 2 or 0
			local function f(u)
				if anim then return 1 - math.abs((u + sh) % 2 - 1) end
				return u
			end
			rc3.ac = a:Lerp(b, f(0))
			if not sch then
				local n = derive(a:Lerp(b, 0.5))
				rc3.bg, rc3.cd, rc3.hv, rc3.pr = n.bg, n.cd, n.hv, n.pr
			end
			perEl = function(role, d)
				if role == "ac" then return a:Lerp(b, f(uAt(d))) end
			end
		elseif mode == "Pulse" then
			local k = (math.sin(now * spd * 3) + 1) / 2
			rc3.ac = base.ac:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), k * 0.5)
		end
		if not animated and not FX.dirty then return end
		FX.dirty = false
		fxPaint(rc3, perEl)
		for _, r in names do
			cT[r] = rc3[r]
			FX.look[key(rc3[r])] = {r, now}
		end
	end
	track(RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not alive() then return end
		if (Flags.fx or "Off") == "Off" then return end
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - FX.lastTick < 0.05 then return end
		FX.lastTick = now
		if not FX.on then fxStart() end
		if now - FX.lastScan > 0.4 then
			FX.lastScan = now
			if fxScan(now) > 0 then FX.dirty = true end
		end
		fxRender(now)
	end))

	local function applyCurrent()
		FX.base = baseTheme()
		FX.dirty = true
		if (Flags.fx or "Off") == "Off" then
			if FX.on then fxStop() end
			applyTheme(FX.base)
		end
	end

	local function applyOpacity()
		local pct = math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.uo) or 100, 1, 100)
		if pct == 100 and not X.opChanged then return end
		X.opChanged = pct ~= 100
		local tr = 1 - pct / 100
		pcall(function() gw.Main.BackgroundTransparency = tr end)
		-- Panels, section headers (the ^ rows), dropdown popups and input boxes all follow the opacity.
		local ks = {[key(cT.cd)] = true, [key(cT.hv)] = true, [key(cT.bg)] = true}
		for _, d in gw.Main:GetDescendants() do
			pcall(function()
				if (d:IsA("Frame") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextLabel")) and d.BackgroundTransparency < 1 and ks[key(d.BackgroundColor3)] and d.AbsoluteSize.X > 20 then
					if d.BackgroundTransparency ~= tr then d.BackgroundTransparency = tr end
				end
			end)
		end
		-- Outer borders never follow the opacity: pin them to their normal look.
		pcall(function()
			local ms = gw.Main:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if ms then ms.Transparency, ms.Thickness = 0.15, 1 end
			stk.Transparency = 0.1
		end)
	end
	-- Re-apply while a custom opacity is active so rows revealed/created later (expanding ^, popups) match.
	task.spawn(function()
		while alive() do
			task.wait(0.5)
			if (tonumber(Flags.uo) or 100) < 100 or X.opChanged then pcall(applyOpacity) end
		end
	end)

	-- --------------------------------------------------------- Setting tab UI
	ts:Section({Name = "Language & Format"})
	ts:Dropdown({Name = "Language", Options = {"English", "Tiếng Việt"}, Default = "English", Flag = "lang", Callback = function(v)
		L:SetLanguage(v == "Tiếng Việt" and "VI" or "EN")
	end})
	local nfModes = {["1 000 000.00"] = "space", ["1.000.000,00"] = "dot", ["1,000,000.00"] = "comma", ["Short (1.5M)"] = "short", ["Myriad (100K = 10 Wan)"] = "van"}
	ts:Dropdown({Name = "Number Format", Options = {"1 000 000.00", "1.000.000,00", "1,000,000.00", "Short (1.5M)", "Myriad (100K = 10 Wan)"}, Default = "1 000 000.00", Flag = "nfm", Callback = function(v)
		ge.__HowNumFmt = nfModes[v] or "space"
	end})
	task.defer(function()
		L:SetLanguage(Flags.lang == "Tiếng Việt" and "VI" or "EN")
		ge.__HowNumFmt = nfModes[Flags.nfm or "1 000 000.00"] or "space"
	end)

	ts:Section({Name = "Overhead Font"})
	local fontOrig = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local function curFont()
		local n = Flags.pnf
		if type(n) ~= "string" or n == "" or n == "Default" then return nil end
		local ok, f = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[n] end)
		return ok and f or nil
	end
	local function fontOne(d, wf)
		if not (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox")) then return end
		local okf, fam = pcall(function() return d.FontFace.Family end)
		if okf and tostring(fam):find("BuilderIcons") then return end
		if wf then
			if fontOrig[d] == nil then fontOrig[d] = d.FontFace end
			if d.Font ~= wf then d.Font = wf end
		elseif fontOrig[d] then
			d.FontFace = fontOrig[d]
			fontOrig[d] = nil
		end
	end
	local function applyAllFont()
		local wf = curFont()
		for _, root in {gw.Gui, hud} do
			for _, d in root:GetDescendants() do pcall(fontOne, d, wf) end
		end
	end
	for _, root in {gw.Gui, hud} do
		track(root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
			local wf = curFont()
			if wf then task.defer(function() pcall(fontOne, d, wf) end) end
		end))
	end
	task.defer(applyAllFont)
	do
		local fonts, names = {"Default"}, {}
		for _, it in Enum.Font:GetEnumItems() do
			if it.Name ~= "Unknown" then table.insert(names, it.Name) end
		end
		table.sort(names)
		for _, n in names do table.insert(fonts, n) end
		ts:Dropdown({Name = "Overhead Font", Options = fonts, Default = "Default", Flag = "pnf", Callback = function() applyAllFont() end})
	end

	ts:Section({Name = "Config"})
	ts:Slider({Name = "Tween Speed", Min = 10, Max = 300, Step = 1, Default = 30, Flag = "tws", Callback = function(v) ge.__HowTS = v end})
	ge.__HowTS = tonumber(Flags.tws) or 30

	ts:Section({Name = "UI Color"})
	local themeDD, palDD
	local function useAuto()
		if palDD and Flags.pal ~= PAL_AUTO then pcall(function() palDD:Set(PAL_AUTO) end) end

	palDD = ts:Dropdown({Name = "Palette", Options = palOpts, Default = PAL_AUTO, Flag = "pal", Callback = function() applyCurrent() end})
	ts:Slider({Name = "Custom Red", Min = 0, Max = 255, Step = 1, Default = 33, Flag = "cr", Callback = function() if Flags.th == "Custom" then useAuto(); applyCurrent() end end})
	ts:Slider({Name = "Custom Green", Min = 0, Max = 255, Step = 1, Default = 150, Flag = "cg", Callback = function() if Flags.th == "Custom" then useAuto(); applyCurrent() end end})
	ts:Slider({Name = "Custom Blue", Min = 0, Max = 255, Step = 1, Default = 243, Flag = "cb", Callback = function() if Flags.th == "Custom" then useAuto(); applyCurrent() end end})
	local hexBox
	hexBox = ts:TextBox({Name = "Custom Hex (#RRGGBB)", Default = "", Placeholder = "#2196F3", Flag = "chx", Callback = function(v)
		local hx = tostring(v):gsub("#", "")
		if #hx == 6 and hx:match("^%x+$") then
			local r, g, b = tonumber(hx:sub(1, 2), 16), tonumber(hx:sub(3, 4), 16), tonumber(hx:sub(5, 6), 16)
			for _, e in {{"cr", r}, {"cg", g}, {"cb", b}} do
				for _, row in ts.Rows do
					if row.Flag == e[1] and row.Set then pcall(function() row:Set(e[2]) end) end
				end
			end
			themeDD:Set("Custom")
			useAuto()
			applyCurrent()
		end
	end})
	ts:Button({Name = "Apply Custom Color", Callback = function()
		themeDD:Set("Custom")
		useAuto()
		applyCurrent()
	end})
	previewRow = ts:Label({Text = "Color preview (accent)"})
	pcall(function() previewRow.Row.BackgroundColor3 = cT.ac end)

	-- Palette: tap a swatch to apply it
	ts:Section({Name = "Color Palette"})
	local function swatchGrid(title, items)
		local lab = ts:Label({Text = title})
		local grid = Instance.new("Frame")
		grid.Name = "HowPalette"
		grid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		grid.BorderSizePixel = 0
		grid.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22)
		grid.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
		grid.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		grid:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
		local gl = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
		gl.CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
		gl.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
		gl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		gl.Parent = grid
		for i, it in items do
			local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
			b.Name = "Swatch"
			b.Text = ""
			b.AutoButtonColor = false
			b.BorderSizePixel = 0
			b.LayoutOrder = i
			b.BackgroundColor3 = it.color
			b:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
			if it.grad then
				b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
				g.Rotation = 45
				g.Color = it.grad
				g.Parent = b
			end
			local cr = Instance.new("UICorner")
			cr.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
			cr.Parent = b
			local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
			st.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			st.Transparency = 0.75
			st.Thickness = 1
			st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
			st:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
			st.Parent = b
			b.Activated:Connect(function() it.pick() end)
			b.Parent = grid
		end
		grid.Parent = lab.Row
		return lab
	end
	do
		local items = {}
		for _, p in presets do
			local nm = p[1]
			table.insert(items, {color = pMap[nm], pick = function()
				useAuto()
				themeDD:Set(nm)
				applyCurrent()
			end})
		end
		swatchGrid("Colors (tap to apply)", items)
		local sitems = {}
		for _, s in schemes do
			local nm, sc = s[1], schemeMap[s[1]]
			table.insert(sitems, {
				color = sc.ac,
				grad = ColorSequence.new({
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, sc.bg), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.49, sc.bg),
					ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.51, sc.ac), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, sc.ac),
				}),
				pick = function()
					palDD:Set(nm)
					applyCurrent()
				end,
			})
		end
		swatchGrid("Palettes (tap to apply)", sitems)
	end

		local v = math.clamp((tonumber(Flags.dmy2) or 80) + d, 5, 95)
		if dmySl then pcall(function() dmySl:Set(v) end) else Flags.dmy2 = v end
		applyDmy()

		gw:ResetStore("cf:")
		gw:Notify({Title = "Risk Warnings", Text = "Reset", Time = 2})
	end})

	ts:Section({Name = "Presets"})
	local slotDD = ts:Dropdown({Name = "Preset Slot", Options = {"Preset 1", "Preset 2", "Preset 3", "Preset 4", "Preset 5"}, Default = "Preset 1"})
	local slotInfo = ts:Label({Text = "-"})
	local function allPresets()
		local v = gw:GetStore("presets")
		return type(v) == "table" and v or {}
	end
	local function refreshSlots()
		local pr, parts = allPresets(), {}
		for i = 1, 5 do
			local sl = pr["Preset " .. i]
			table.insert(parts, i .. ": " .. ((sl and sl.t) or "-"))
		end
		slotInfo:Set(table.concat(parts, "  |  "))
	end
	refreshSlots()
	ts:Button({Name = "Save To Slot", Callback = function()
		local k = slotDD:Get() or "Preset 1"
		local pr = allPresets()
		pr[k] = {t = os.date("%d/%m %H:%M"), d = gw:Snapshot()}
		gw:SetStore("presets", pr)
		refreshSlots()
		gw:Notify({Title = "Presets", Text = k .. " saved", Time = 2})
	end})
	ts:Button({Name = "Load Slot", Callback = function()
		local k = slotDD:Get() or "Preset 1"
		local sl = allPresets()[k]
		if not sl or type(sl.d) ~= "table" then
			gw:Notify({Title = "Presets", Text = k .. " is empty", Time = 2})
			return
		end
		local n = gw:ApplySnapshot(sl.d)
		gw:Notify({Title = "Presets", Text = k .. " loaded (" .. n .. ")", Time = 3})
	end})
	ts:Button({Name = "Clear Slot", Callback = function()
		local k = slotDD:Get() or "Preset 1"
		local pr = allPresets()
		pr[k] = nil
		gw:SetStore("presets", pr)
		refreshSlots()
		gw:Notify({Title = "Presets", Text = k .. " cleared", Time = 2})
	end})

	-- apply saved visuals once everything exists
	task.defer(function()
		applyCurrent()
		applyOpacity()
	end)

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ Stats tab
	local tst = gw:Tab({Name = "Stats", Icon = "trophy"})
	tst:Section({Name = "Fishing Statistics"})
	local ovL = tst:Label({Text = "..."})
	tst:Section({Name = "Caught By Rarity"})
	local raL = tst:Label({Text = "..."})
	tst:Button({Name = "Reset Time / Fish / Money", Callback = resetStats})
	task.spawn(function()
		while alive() do
			pcall(function()
				local n, l, cap = bagCounts()
				local perH = S.t >= 5 and S.fish / (S.t / 3600) or 0
				local avg = S.fish > 0 and S.t / S.fish or 0
				setL(ovL, table.concat({
					"Fishing Time: " .. fmtT(S.t),
					"Fish Caught: " .. S.fish,
					"Boss Caught: " .. S.boss,
					string.format("Fish / Hour: %.1f", perH),
					string.format("Avg Time / Fish: %.1fs", avg),
					"Total Earned: " .. fmtN(S.money),
					"Coin / Hour: " .. fmtN(moneyPerHour()),
					"Last Sell: +" .. fmtN(S.last),
					"Total Damage: " .. fmtN(S.tdmg),
					"DPS (Live): " .. fmtN(dps(), 2),
					"DPS (Avg): " .. fmtN(dpsAvg(), 2),
					`In Bag: {bagStr(n, cap)} | Locked: {l}`,
				}, "\n"))
				local out = {}
				for _, r in ra do
					local c = S.rar[r] or 0
					table.insert(out, string.format("%s: %d (%.0f%%)", r, c, S.fish > 0 and c / S.fish * 100 or 0))
				end
				for r, c in S.rar do
					if not table.find(ra, r) then table.insert(out, `{r}: {c}`) end
				end
				setL(raL, table.concat(out, "\n"))
			end)
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	-- ----------------------------------------------------------- Player tab
	local tp = gw:Tab({Name = "Player", Icon = "person"})
	local PL = {speed = 16, jump = 50}

	local function numPair(title, lo, hi, def, fl, key2)
		local sl, tx
		local busy = false
		sl = tp:Slider({Name = title .. " Slider", Min = lo, Max = hi, Step = 1, Default = def, Flag = fl .. "s", Callback = function(v)
			PL[key2] = v
			if not busy then
				busy = true
				pcall(function() tx:Set(tostring(v)) end)
				busy = false
			end
		end})
		tx = tp:TextBox({Name = title .. " (Type Number)", Default = tostring(def), Placeholder = tostring(def), Flag = fl .. "t", Callback = function(v)
			local n = tonumber(v)
			if not n then return end
			n = math.clamp(n, lo, hi)
			PL[key2] = n
			if not busy then
				busy = true
				pcall(function() sl:Set(math.min(n, hi)) end)
				busy = false
			end
		end})
		PL[key2] = math.clamp(tonumber(Flags[fl .. "t"]) or tonumber(Flags[fl .. "s"]) or def, lo, hi)
	end

	tp:Section({Name = "Speed / Jump"})
	numPair("Walk Speed", 16, 200, 16, "pws", "speed")
	tp:Toggle({Name = "Enable Walk Speed", Confirm = "This feature changes your character's movement and can be detected by the game. Enable anyway?", Flag = "pse", Callback = function(v)
		if not v then
			local _, _, h = lc()
			if h then h.WalkSpeed = 16 end
		end
	end})
	numPair("Jump Power", 50, 200, 50, "pjp", "jump")
	tp:Toggle({Name = "Enable Jump Power", Confirm = "This feature changes your character's movement and can be detected by the game. Enable anyway?", Flag = "pje", Callback = function(v)
		if not v then
			local _, _, h = lc()
			if h then h.JumpPower = 50 end
		end
	end})
	track(RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local _, _, h = lc()
		if not h then return end
		if Flags.pse == true then h.WalkSpeed = PL.speed end
		if Flags.pje == true then
			h.UseJumpPower = true
			h.JumpPower = PL.jump
		end
	end))

	tp:Section({Name = "Movement"})
	tp:Toggle({Name = "Infinite Jump", Confirm = "This feature changes your character's movement and can be detected by the game. Enable anyway?", Flag = "pij"})
	tp:Toggle({Name = "Noclip", Confirm = "This feature changes your character's movement and can be detected by the game. Enable anyway?", Flag = "pnc"})
	track(UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
		if Flags.pij ~= true then return end
		local _, _, h = lc()
		if h then h:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
	end))
	track(RS.Stepped:Connect(function()
		if Flags.pnc ~= true then return end
		local c = lp.Character
		if not c then return end
		for _, p in c:GetDescendants() do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.CanCollide then p.CanCollide = false end
		end
	end))

	tp:Section({Name = "Name (only you can see)"})
	tp:TextBox({Name = "Overhead Name", Default = "", Placeholder = "Custom name", Flag = "pnm"})
	local function restoreName()
		local c = lp.Character
		if not c then return end
		local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if h then h.DisplayName = lp.DisplayName end
		for _, d in c:GetDescendants() do
			if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d:GetAttribute("HowOrig") then
				d.Text = d:GetAttribute("HowOrig")
				d:SetAttribute("HowOrig", nil)
			end
		end
	end
	tp:Toggle({Name = "Apply Overhead Name", Flag = "pna", Callback = function(v)
		if not v then pcall(restoreName) end
	end})
	task.spawn(function()
		while alive() do
			pcall(function()
				local want = tostring(Flags.pnm or "")
				local c = lp.Character
				if Flags.pna == true and want ~= "" and c then
					local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					if h and h.DisplayName ~= want then h.DisplayName = want end
					for _, d in c:GetDescendants() do
						if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("BillboardGui") then
							local orig = d:GetAttribute("HowOrig")
							if orig or d.Text == lp.Name or d.Text == lp.DisplayName then
								if not orig then d:SetAttribute("HowOrig", d.Text) end
								if d.Text ~= want then d.Text = want end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

	-- ------------------------------------------------------------ tab icons
	task.spawn(function()
		local want = {
			Player = {"heart", "star", "people", "users", "group", "person", "user"},
			Stats = {"chart-bar", "bar-chart", "chart-line-up", "analytics", "chart-pie", "trophy"},
			General = {"house", "home", "fishing-hook", "anchor", "fish", "shrimp"},
			Status = {"trophy", "crown", "flag", "clipboard-list", "target", "clipboard"},
			Misc = {"package", "box", "cube", "puzzle-piece", "toolbox", "wrench", "dice", "four-squares-grid"},
			Shop = {"shopping-cart", "cart", "shopping-basket", "storefront", "store", "shopping-bag", "bag", "coins"},
			Teleport = {"key", "rocket", "paper-plane", "globe", "map-pin", "compass", "arrows-out", "person-teleport"},
			Visual = {"eye", "palette", "paint-brush", "sparkle"},
			Setting = {"gear", "gear-six", "sliders"},
		}
		local ts2 = game:GetService("TextService")
		local used = {}
		local function iconLab(T)
			for _, d in T.Btn:GetChildren() do
				if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.FontFace.Family:find("BuilderIcons") then return d end
			end
		end
		for _, T in gw.Tabs do
			local l0 = iconLab(T)
			if l0 then used[l0.Text] = T end
		end
		for _, T in gw.Tabs do
			local cands = want[T.Name]
			local lab = iconLab(T)
			if cands and lab then
				local function wid(nm)
					local ok, b = pcall(function()
						local pr = Instance.new("GetTextBoundsParams")
						pr.Text, pr.Font, pr.Size, pr.Width = nm, lab.FontFace, 16, 1000
						return ts2:GetTextBoundsAsync(pr)
					end)
					return ok and b and b.X or nil
				end
				-- A real icon renders as one glyph with the same width as a known-good icon.
				-- (Unknown names can measure 0 wide and used to be picked by mistake, leaving the icon blank.)
				local ref = wid("trophy")
				if ref and ref > 4 then
					for _, nm in cands do
						local w = wid(nm)
						if w and math.abs(w - ref) <= 4 and (used[nm] == nil or used[nm] == T) then
							if used[lab.Text] == T then used[lab.Text] = nil end
							lab.Text = nm
							used[nm] = T
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, T in gw.Tabs do
			local l1 = iconLab(T)
			if l1 then warn("[HowHub] tab icon " .. T.Name .. " = " .. l1.Text) end
		end
	end)

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ tab icons (PNG pack)

	}
	if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then return end
	local A = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
	local rev = {}
	for i = 1, 64 do rev[A:sub(i, i)] = i - 1 end
	local function dec(s)
		local out, n, bits = {}, 0, 0
		for ch in s:gmatch("[%w%+/]") do
			n = n * 64 + rev[ch]
			bits += 6
			if bits >= 8 then
				bits -= 8
				local v = math.floor(n / 2 ^ bits)
				out[#out + 1] = string.char(v)
				n -= v * 2 ^ bits
			end
		end
		return table.concat(out)
	end
	pcall(function() if makefolder and not (isfolder and isfolder("HowHub")) then makefolder("HowHub") end end)
	task.wait(1.5)
	for _, T in gw.Tabs do
		local b64 = data[T.Name]
		local lab
		for _, d in T.Btn:GetChildren() do
			if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.FontFace.Family:find("BuilderIcons") then lab = d break end
		end
		if b64 and lab then
			pcall(function()
				local path = "HowHub/ico_" .. T.Name .. ".png"
				writefile(path, dec(b64))
				local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
				img.Name, img.BackgroundTransparency, img.Active = "PngIcon", 1, false
				img.AnchorPoint, img.Position, img.Size = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
				img.Image, img.ImageColor3 = getcustomasset(path), lab.TextColor3
				img.Parent = T.Btn
				lab.TextTransparency = 1
				lab:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(function() img.ImageColor3 = lab.TextColor3 end)
			end)
		end
	end
end)

-- ------------------------------------------------------------ face camera direction
task.spawn(function()
	local Rs, Pl = game:GetService("RunService"), game:GetService("Players")
	local wasOn, hum0 = false, nil
	local cn
	cn = Rs.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if X.dead then cn:Disconnect() return end
		local on = getgenv().__HowFaceCam == true
		local ch = Pl.LocalPlayer.Character
		local hrp = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if on and hrp and hum then
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local look = cam and Vector3.new(cam.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, cam.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			if look and look.Magnitude > 0.01 then
				hum.AutoRotate = false
				hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAlong(hrp.Position, look.Unit)
				wasOn, hum0 = true, hum
			end
		elseif wasOn then
			wasOn = false
			pcall(function() if hum0 then hum0.AutoRotate = true end end)
		end
	end)
end)
end
__howf() end
do
	local okf, errf = pcall(function() gw:EnableFavorites() end)
	if not okf then warn("Favorites Failed: " .. tostring(errf)) end
	pcall(function() gw:Ready() end)
end
ge.__HowLoaded = true
